```APIDOC
## POST /mythiccraft/location/safe-spawn

### Description
Finds a safe location to spawn a mob within a given radius and height constraints.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/mythiccraft/location/safe-spawn

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
- **base** (AbstractLocation) - Required - The base location to search from.
- **radiusXZ** (double) - Required - The radius along the X and Z axes.
- **radiusY** (double) - Required - The radius along the Y axis.
- **mob_height** (int) - Required - The height of the mob.
- **yMod** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to modify the Y coordinate.
- **onSurface** (boolean) - Optional - Whether to spawn on the surface.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "base": {"world": "world", "x": 0.0, "y": 64.0, "z": 0.0},
  "radiusXZ": 10.0,
  "radiusY": 5.0,
  "mob_height": 2,
  "yMod": true,
  "onSurface": true
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **safeLocation** (AbstractLocation) - A safe location to spawn the mob.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "safeLocation": {"world": "world", "x": 1.5, "y": 65.2, "z": -3.8}
}
```
```

### BukkitFox Constructor and Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/entities/BukkitFox

Provides details on the constructor and key methods of the BukkitFox class, including instantiation, spawning, applying options, comparing entities, and getting height.

```APIDOC
## BukkitFox Class Documentation

### Description
Represents a Fox entity within the MythicCraft Bukkit environment. This class extends `BukkitEntityType` and implements `TameableEntityType`, providing specific functionalities for fox entities.

### Constructor

*   **BukkitFox()**
    *   Description: Initializes a new instance of the `BukkitFox` class.

### Methods

#### `instantiate(MythicConfig mc)`

*   **Method**: `void`
*   **Description**: Instantiates the BukkitFox entity using a provided `MythicConfig`.
*   **Specified by**: `instantiate` in class `MythicEntity`

#### `spawn(Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<Entity> prespawnFunc)`

*   **Method**: `Entity`
*   **Description**: Spawns a BukkitFox entity at the specified location with given reason and an optional prespawn function.
*   **Specified by**: `spawn` in class `BukkitEntityType`

#### `applyOptions(Entity entity)`

*   **Method**: `Entity`
*   **Description**: Applies specific options to an existing Bukkit entity, customizing its behavior as a BukkitFox.
*   **Specified by**: `applyOptions` in class `BukkitEntityType`

#### `compare(Entity e)`

*   **Method**: `boolean`
*   **Description**: Compares the current BukkitFox entity with another Bukkit entity to determine equality or compatibility.
*   **Specified by**: `compare` in class `BukkitEntityType`

#### `getHeight()`

*   **Method**: `int`
*   **Description**: Retrieves the height of the BukkitFox entity.
*   **Overrides**: `getHeight` in class `MythicEntity`

### Inherited Methods

*   **From `BukkitEntityType`**: `getHead`, `getHeadString`, `MythicEntity getMythicEntity(...)`, `spawn(...)`, `spawnBaseEntity`, `spawnEntity`
*   **From `Object`**: `clone`, `equals`, `finalize`, `getClass`, `hashCode`, `notify`, `notifyAll`, `toString`, `wait`
*   **From `TameableEntityType`**: `getTameableProperty`
```

### BukkitFirework Constructor and Launch Method

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/adapters/BukkitFirework

This section details the constructor for BukkitFirework and its launch method, including parameters and their descriptions.

```APIDOC
## BukkitFirework API

### Description
Provides functionality to create and launch fireworks within the Bukkit Minecraft server environment.

### Constructor
`BukkitFirework(String type, int duration, Collection<Color> colors, Collection<Color> fadeColors, boolean flicker, boolean trail)`

**Parameters:**
- **type** (String) - The type of firework effect.
- **duration** (int) - The duration of the firework effect.
- **colors** (Collection<Color>) - A collection of colors for the firework.
- **fadeColors** (Collection<Color>) - A collection of fade colors for the firework.
- **flicker** (boolean) - Whether the firework should flicker.
- **trail** (boolean) - Whether the firework should leave a trail.

### Method
`void launch(SkillMetadata data, AbstractLocation location)`

**Description:**
Launches the firework at the specified location with the given skill metadata.

**Parameters:**
- **data** (SkillMetadata) - Metadata associated with the skill that is launching the firework.
- **location** (AbstractLocation) - The location where the firework should be launched.
```

### MythicMob Configuration API

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/mobs/MobType

Methods for getting and setting various configurations of a MythicMob.

```APIDOC
## GET/POST/PUT /websites/mythiccraft_io_javadocs_mythic/config

### Description
This section covers methods for retrieving and modifying MythicMob configurations, including entity type, threat table settings, and skill timers.

### Method
GET, POST, PUT (depending on the specific configuration operation)

### Endpoint
/websites/mythiccraft_io_javadocs_mythic/config

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters (Examples)
- **entityType** (MythicEntityType) - Used with `setEntityType()`
- **threatTableDecaysUnreachable** (boolean) - Used with `getThreatTableDecaysUnreachable()`
- **threatTableUseDamageTaken** (boolean) - Used with `getThreatTableUseDamageTaken()`
- **timerSkills** (Queue<SkillMechanic>) - Used with `getTimerSkills()`
- **spawnVelocityY** (double) - Used with `getSpawnVelocityY()`
- **spawnVelocityZ** (double) - Used with `getSpawnVelocityZ()`

#### Request Body (for setting configurations)
- **fieldName** (type) - Value for the configuration field

### Request Example (Setting Entity Type)
```json
{

  "spawnVelocityY": 0.5
}
```

## POST /mythicmobs/spawn

### Description

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/mythicmobs/spawn

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **location** (AbstractLocation) - Required - The location to spawn the mob.

- **reason** (SpawnReason) - Optional - The reason for spawning.
- **prespawnFunc** (Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>) - Optional - A function to execute before the mob spawns.
- **spawner** (MythicSpawner) - Optional - The spawner associated with this mob.

(Not applicable for this endpoint, parameters are passed via query)

The instantiate method takes a MythicConfig to set up the entity, while spawn allows for entity creation at a specific location with optional pre-spawn logic. applyOptions modifies entity properties, and compare checks for entity equality.

  public void instantiate(MythicConfig mc) {
  }

  public org.bukkit.entity.Entity spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc) {
  }

  public org.bukkit.entity.Entity applyOptions(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e) {
  }

  public boolean compare(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e) {
  }
}

## BukkitEntity API

### Description
This section covers the core functionalities of the `BukkitEntity` class, including its constructor and essential methods for entity manipulation.

### Constructor
```APIDOC
BukkitEntity(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e)
```
* **Description**: Initializes a new `BukkitEntity` instance with a given Bukkit entity.

### Methods
#### Damage
* **Method**: `damage(float damage)`
* **Description**: Applies a specified amount of damage to the entity.

#### Removal
* **Method**: `remove()`
* **Description**: Removes the entity from the game world.

#### Metadata
* **Method**: `getMetadata(String key)`
* **Returns**: `Optional<Object>`
* **Description**: Retrieves metadata associated with a specific key.

* **Method**: `hasMetadata(String key)`
* **Returns**: `boolean`
* **Description**: Checks if the entity has metadata associated with a specific key.

* **Method**: `setMetadata(String key, Object value)`
* **Description**: Sets a metadata value for the entity under a given key.

* **Method**: `removeMetadata(String key)`
* **Description**: Removes metadata associated with a specific key.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "example": "BukkitEntity myEntity = new BukkitEntity(someBukkitEntity);"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
* **Description**: Methods typically return `void` or `boolean` for most operations. Data retrieval methods return specific types or Optional wrappers.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "example": "// No direct response body for these methods, results are in return values or side effects."
}
```
```

### MythicLineConfig Interface: Retrieving String Values (Java)

This snippet demonstrates how to retrieve string values from MythicLineConfig. It supports getting a value by a single key, with a default value, or with multiple fallback keys. It also shows how to get a string list with or without default values and fallback keys.

```Java
String getString(String key);
String getString(String key, String def);
String getString(String[] key);
String getString(String[] key, String def, String... args);
List<String> getStringList(String key, String def);
List<String> getStringList(String[] key, String def);
List<String> getStringList(String[] key, String def, String... args);

```APIDOC
## BukkitGiant Class Documentation

### Description
Provides a Bukkit-specific implementation for the MythicGiant entity, handling its instantiation, spawning, and configuration within the Bukkit environment.

### Methods

#### Constructor

*   **BukkitGiant()**
    *   Description: Initializes a new instance of the BukkitGiant class.

#### Core Methods

*   **instantiate(MythicConfig mc)**
    *   Method: `void`
    *   Description: Instantiates the BukkitGiant entity using the provided MythicConfig.
    *   Inherited from: `MythicEntity`

*   **spawn(Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<Entity> prespawnFunc)**
    *   Method: `Entity`
    *   Description: Spawns a BukkitGiant entity at the specified location with the given spawn reason and an optional prespawn function.
    *   Inherited from: `BukkitEntityType`

*   **applyOptions(Entity entity)**
    *   Method: `Entity`
    *   Description: Applies specific Bukkit-related options to an already spawned Bukkit entity.
    *   Inherited from: `BukkitEntityType`

*   **compare(Entity e)**
    *   Method: `boolean`
    *   Description: Compares this BukkitGiant instance with another Bukkit entity.
    *   Inherited from: `BukkitEntityType`

### Inherited Methods

*   **From `BukkitEntityType`**: `getHead`, `getHeadString`, `getMythicEntity`, `spawn`, `spawnBaseEntity`, `spawnEntity`
*   **From `MythicEntity`**: `getHeight`
*   **From `Object`**: `clone`, `equals`, `finalize`, `getClass`, `hashCode`, `notify`, `notifyAll`, `toString`, `wait`
```

### Timing Constructor and Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/utils/Timing

Details on how to initialize and use the Timing utility for performance tracking.

```APIDOC
## Timing Class Documentation

### Description
The `Timing` class is a utility for measuring the execution time of specific code blocks or operations within the MythicCraft system. It allows developers to track performance and identify potential bottlenecks.

### Class Structure

#### Nested Classes
- **`Timing.TimingResults`**: A static nested class that likely holds the results of timing measurements.

### Constructor Summary

#### `Timing(String type)`
- **Description**: Initializes a new `Timing` instance with a specified type or name for the timing measurement.
- **Method**: `public Timing(String type)`
- **Parameters**:
  - `type` (String) - Required - The identifier for this timing measurement.

### Method Summary

#### Instance Methods

##### `end()`
- **Description**: Marks the end of the timing measurement for the current instance.
- **Method**: `public void end()`

##### `getNanoTime()`
- **Description**: Retrieves the elapsed time in nanoseconds since the timing measurement started or the last `end()` call.
- **Method**: `public long getNanoTime()`
- **Returns**: (long) - The elapsed time in nanoseconds.

#### Static Methods

##### `getTimings()`
- **Description**: Retrieves a map containing all active or completed timing results.
- **Method**: `public static HashMap<String,Timing.TimingResults> getTimings()`
- **Returns**: (HashMap<String, Timing.TimingResults>) - A map where keys are timing identifiers and values are `TimingResults` objects.

### Inherited Methods from `java.lang.Object`
- `clone`, `equals`, `finalize`, `getClass`, `hashCode`, `notify`, `notifyAll`, `toString`, `wait`, `wait`, `wait`
```

    *   **Description**: Gets the height of the Allay entity.
    *   **Method**: `int`
    *   **Overrides**: `getHeight` in class `MythicEntity`.

### Inherited Methods from BukkitEntityType

*   `getHead()`
*   `getHeadString()`
*   `getMythicEntity()`
*   `getMythicEntity(MythicConfig mc)`
*   `getMythicEntity(MythicConfig mc, Consumer<MythicEntity> setupFunc)`
*   `spawn(Location location)`
*   `spawn(Location location, Consumer<Entity> prespawnFunc)`
*   `spawn(Location location, SpawnReason reason)`
*   `spawnBaseEntity(Location location)`
*   `spawnEntity(Location location, EntityType type)`

### Inherited Methods from Object

*   `clone()`
*   `equals(Object obj)`
*   `finalize()`
*   `getClass()`
*   `hashCode()`
*   `notify()`
*   `notifyAll()`
*   `toString()`
*   `wait()`
*   `wait(long timeout)`
*   `wait(long timeout, int nanos)`
```

### StaticFloat get Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/skills/placeholders/parsers/StaticFloat

Methods for retrieving the float value represented by the StaticFloat object, with variations for different contexts.

```APIDOC
## StaticFloat get Methods

### Description
Retrieves the float value associated with this placeholder.

### Methods

*   **`get()`**
    *   Returns: `float` - The static float value.
    *   Description: Returns the pre-defined float value.

*   **`get(AbstractEntity entity)`**
    *   Parameters:
        *   **entity** (`AbstractEntity`) - The entity context.
    *   Returns: `float` - The float value evaluated in the context of the entity.
    *   Description: Evaluates and returns the float value using the provided entity.

*   **`get(SkillCaster caster)`**
    *   Parameters:
        *   **caster** (`SkillCaster`) - The caster context.
    *   Returns: `float` - The float value evaluated in the context of the caster.
    *   Description: Evaluates and returns the float value using the provided skill caster.

*   **`get(PlaceholderMeta meta)`**
    *   Parameters:
        *   **meta** (`PlaceholderMeta`) - The placeholder metadata.
    *   Returns: `float` - The float value evaluated with the given metadata.
    *   Description: Evaluates and returns the float value using the provided placeholder metadata.

*   **`get(PlaceholderMeta meta, AbstractEntity entity)`**
    *   Parameters:
        *   **meta** (`PlaceholderMeta`) - The placeholder metadata.
        *   **entity** (`AbstractEntity`) - The entity context.
    *   Returns: `float` - The float value evaluated with both metadata and entity context.
    *   Description: Evaluates and returns the float value using both placeholder metadata and entity context.
```

### MythicMob Constructors and Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/api/mobs/class-use/MythicMob

This section details the constructors and methods related to the MythicMob class, showing how it's created and used in various contexts.

```APIDOC
## MythicMob Class Operations

### Description
This section provides information on how MythicMob objects are constructed and utilized across different parts of the MythicCraft library.

### Method
N/A (Documentation of class methods and constructors)

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
N/A

#### Query Parameters
N/A

#### Request Body
N/A

### Request Example
N/A

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
N/A

#### Response Example
N/A

### Constructors
#### `ActiveMob(AbstractEntity entity, MythicMob type, double level)`
- **entity** (AbstractEntity) - The entity associated with the active mob.
- **type** (MythicMob) - The type of the MythicMob.
- **level** (double) - The level of the MythicMob.

#### `MobModel(MythicMob baseMob, MythicConfig config)`
- **baseMob** (MythicMob) - The base MythicMob object.
- **config** (MythicConfig) - The configuration for the mob model.

#### `MPetsModel(MythicMob baseMob, MythicConfig config)`
- **baseMob** (MythicMob) - The base MythicMob object.
- **config** (MythicConfig) - The configuration for the MPets model.

### Methods Returning MythicMob
#### `MobExecutor.determineMobType(AbstractEntity l)`
- Returns the MythicMob type of the given entity.

#### `MobExecutor.getMythicMobByDisplayCompat(AbstractEntity l)`
- Returns the MythicMob by its display name, with compatibility checks.

#### `EggManager.getMythicMobFromEgg(String s)`
- Deprecated. Retrieves a MythicMob from a string identifier, likely representing an egg.

#### `EggManager.getMythicMobFromEgg(org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack eggItem)`
- Retrieves a MythicMob from an ItemStack representing an egg.

#### `ActiveMob.getType()`
- Returns the MythicMob type of the active mob.

### Methods Returning Collections of MythicMob
#### `MobExecutor.getMobTypes()`
- Returns a collection of all loaded MythicMob types.

#### `MobExecutor.getVanillaTypes()`
- Returns a collection of all Vanilla overrides for MythicMob types.

### Methods with MythicMob Parameters
#### `MobType.compareTo(MythicMob mm)`
- Compares this MythicMob type with another.

#### `MobExecutor.getMythicMobLevel(MythicMob mm, AbstractEntity l)`
- Gets the level of a MythicMob associated with an entity.

#### `EggManager.giveMythicEgg(MythicMob mm, org.bukkit.entity.Player player, int amount)`
- Gives a specified amount of MythicMob eggs to a player.

#### `MobExecutor.registerActiveMob(AbstractEntity l, MythicMob mm, double level)`
- Registers an active MythicMob with its entity, type, and level.

#### `MobExecutor.SetupMythicMobCompat(org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity l, MythicMob mm)`
- Sets up compatibility for a LivingEntity with a MythicMob.

#### `MythicSpawner.setType(MythicMob type)`
- Sets the specific MythicMob type that this spawner will spawn.

#### `MythicSpawner.setTypes(Collection<MythicMob> types)`
- Sets a collection of MythicMob types that this spawner can spawn.

### Fields Declared as MythicMob
#### `ParticleEffect.mm`
- Protected field holding a MythicMob instance.

#### `SummonMechanic.mm`
- Protected field holding a MythicMob instance.

#### `SummonPassengerMechanic.mount`
- Protected field holding a MythicMob instance for a passenger.

#### `MobBullet.bulletMob`
- Protected field holding the MythicMob associated with a bullet.

### Methods Using MythicMob in Type Arguments
#### `MobExecutor.getMythicMob(String type)`
- Retrieves an Optional MythicMob by its type name.

#### `MobExecutor.getVanillaType(MythicEntityType type)`
- Returns the Vanilla override for the given MythicEntityType if loaded.

#### `TemplateUtil.handleTemplates(List<TemplateUtil.TemplateMob> mobTemplates, Map<String,MythicMob> mmList)`
- Handles mob templates, likely processing them with a map of MythicMob instances.
```

### BukkitTadpole Constructor and Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/entities/BukkitTadpole

This section details the constructors and methods available for the BukkitTadpole class, including instantiation, spawning, and entity options.

```APIDOC
## BukkitTadpole API

### Description
Provides methods for creating and managing Tadpole entities within the MythicCraft Bukkit environment. This class extends `BukkitEntityType` and inherits functionalities for entity handling.

### Methods

#### Constructor

- **BukkitTadpole()**
  - Description: Initializes a new instance of the `BukkitTadpole` class.

#### Core Methods

- **instantiate(MythicConfig mc)**
  - Description: Instantiates the entity based on the provided MythicConfig.
  - Method: `void`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Class method)

- **spawn(Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<Entity> prespawnFunc)**
  - Description: Spawns a Tadpole entity at the specified location with given spawn reason and a pre-spawn function.
  - Method: `org.bukkit.entity.Entity`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Class method)

- **applyOptions(Entity entity)**
  - Description: Applies various options to an existing Bukkit entity.
  - Method: `org.bukkit.entity.Entity`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Class method)

- **compare(Entity e)**
  - Description: Compares this entity with another Bukkit entity.
  - Method: `boolean`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Class method)

- **getHeight()**
  - Description: Gets the height of the Tadpole entity.
  - Method: `int`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Class method)

### Inherited Methods

Methods inherited from `io.lumine.mythic.bukkit.adapters.BukkitEntityType` include `getHead`, `getHeadString`, `MythicEntity getMythicEntity(...)`, `spawn(...)`, `spawnBaseEntity`, and `spawnEntity`.

Methods inherited from `java.lang.Object` include `clone`, `equals`, `finalize`, `getClass`, `hashCode`, `notify`, `notifyAll`, `toString`, `wait`, and `wait`.
```

```APIDOC
## StatExecution Enum

### Description
An enum representing different execution states or types within the MythicCraft API.

### Methods
#### `static StatExecution valueOf(String name)`
Returns the enum constant of this class with the specified name.

- **name** (String) - The name of the enum constant to return.

#### `static StatExecution[] values()`
Returns an array containing the constants of this enum class, in the order they are declared.

### Usage in Packages
#### `io.lumine.mythic.api.skills.stats`
This package utilizes the `StatExecution` enum.

#### `io.lumine.mythic.core.skills.stats`
In this package, `StatExecution` is used as a field type:

- **Field**: `StatType.executionPoint`
  - **Type**: `protected StatExecution`
  - **Description**: Represents the execution point of a stat.
```

## Method: spawn

`public org.bukkit.entity.Entity spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`

### Description
Spawns a Bee entity at the specified location. Allows for custom prespawn logic.

### Parameters

*   **location** (`org.bukkit.Location`) - The location where the entity will spawn.
*   **reason** (`SpawnReason`) - The reason for spawning the entity.
*   **prespawnFunc** (`Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>`) - A function to execute before the entity is fully spawned.

### Specified by
`spawn` in class `BukkitEntityType`

### SelectorType Enum Documentation

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/skills/targeters/class-use/SelectorType

Details the SelectorType enum, including its methods for getting the type, converting from string, and retrieving all values.

```APIDOC
## SelectorType Enum

### Description
The `SelectorType` enum represents different types of selectors used within the MythicCraft core skills targeting system.

### Methods

#### `getType()`
- **Description**: Returns the `SelectorType` of the current object.
- **Type**: `SelectorType`

#### `valueOf(String name)`
- **Description**: Returns the enum constant of this class with the specified name.
- **Type**: `static SelectorType`
- **Parameters**:
  - **name** (String) - The name of the enum constant to return.

#### `values()`
- **Description**: Returns an array containing the constants of this enum class, in the order they are declared.
- **Type**: `static SelectorType[]`

### Usage Examples

#### Getting the Type of a Targeter
```java
SelectorType type = targeter.getType();
```

#### Converting from a String Name
```java
SelectorType type = SelectorType.valueOf("RADIUS");
```

#### Iterating Through All Selector Types
```java
for (SelectorType type : SelectorType.values()) {
    System.out.println(type.name());
}
```

### Related Classes
- `io.lumine.mythic.core.skills.targeters.IPathSelector`
- `io.lumine.mythic.core.skills.targeters.MPEntity`
- `io.lumine.mythic.core.skills.targeters.MPLocation`
- `io.lumine.mythic.core.skills.targeters.MPMeta`
```

## BukkitFrog API

### Description
This section details the constructors and methods available for the `BukkitFrog` class, which represents a frog entity within the MythicCraft Bukkit API.

#### `BukkitFrog()`

- **Description**: Initializes a new instance of the `BukkitFrog` class.
- **Method**: `public BukkitFrog()`

### Methods

#### `instantiate(MythicConfig mc)`

- **Description**: Instantiates the `BukkitFrog` entity using the provided `MythicConfig`.
- **Method**: `public void instantiate(MythicConfig mc)`
- **Inheritance**: Specified by `instantiate` in class `MythicEntity`.

#### `spawn(Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<Entity> prespawnFunc)`

- **Description**: Spawns a `BukkitFrog` entity at the specified location with given spawn reason and a pre-spawn function.
- **Method**: `public org.bukkit.entity.Entity spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`
- **Inheritance**: Specified by `spawn` in class `BukkitEntityType`.

#### `applyOptions(Entity entity)`

- **Description**: Applies specific options to an existing `BukkitEntity`.
- **Method**: `public org.bukkit.entity.Entity applyOptions(org.bukkit.entity.Entity entity)`
- **Inheritance**: Specified by `applyOptions` in class `BukkitEntityType`.

#### `compare(Entity e)`

- **Description**: Compares this `BukkitFrog` entity with another `BukkitEntity`.
- **Method**: `public boolean compare(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e)`
- **Inheritance**: Specified by `compare` in class `BukkitEntityType`.

#### `getHeight()`

- **Description**: Retrieves the height of the `BukkitFrog` entity.
- **Method**: `public int getHeight()`
- **Overrides**: `getHeight` in class `MythicEntity`.

### BukkitPigZombieVillager Constructor and Methods

Details on the constructors and methods available for the BukkitPigZombieVillager class, including instantiation, spawning entities, applying options, comparison, checking instance type, and getting height.

```APIDOC
## BukkitPigZombieVillager

### Description
Represents a Bukkit entity adapter for a Pig Zombie Villager mob within the MythicCraft framework.

### Method
* **Constructor**: `BukkitPigZombieVillager()`
* **instantiate**: `void instantiate(MythicConfig mc)`
* **spawn**: `org.bukkit.entity.Entity spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`
* **applyOptions**: `org.bukkit.entity.Entity applyOptions(org.bukkit.entity.Entity entity)`
* **compare**: `boolean compare(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e)`
* **isInstanceOf**: `static boolean isInstanceOf(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e)`
* **getHeight**: `int getHeight()`

### Parameters
#### Constructor
* **`BukkitPigZombieVillager()`**: No parameters.

#### `instantiate` Method
* **`mc`** (MythicConfig) - Required - The MythicConfig object containing configuration for the entity.

#### `spawn` Method
* **`location`** (org.bukkit.Location) - Required - The location where the entity should spawn.
* **`reason`** (SpawnReason) - Required - The reason for spawning the entity.
* **`prespawnFunc`** (Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>) - Optional - A function to execute before the entity is fully spawned.

#### `applyOptions` Method
* **`entity`** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - Required - The Bukkit entity to apply options to.

#### `compare` Method
* **`e`** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - Required - The entity to compare against.

#### `isInstanceOf` Method
* **`e`** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - Required - The entity to check.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "mc": "MythicConfig Object Placeholder",
  "location": {
    "world": "world",
    "x": 0.0,
    "y": 64.0,
    "z": 0.0
  },
  "reason": "CUSTOM",
  "prespawnFunc": "Consumer Function Placeholder"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
* **`instantiate`**: No explicit return value (void).
* **`spawn`**: Returns the spawned `org.bukkit.entity.Entity`.
* **`applyOptions`**: Returns the modified `org.bukkit.entity.Entity`.
* **`compare`**: Returns `boolean` indicating if the entities are comparable.
* **`isInstanceOf`**: Returns `boolean` indicating if the entity is an instance of BukkitPigZombieVillager.
* **`getHeight`**: Returns `int` representing the height of the entity.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "spawnedEntity": {
    "type": "PIG_ZOMBIE_VILLAGER",
    "location": {"world": "world", "x": 0.0, "y": 64.0, "z": 0.0}
  }
}
```
```

### FriendlyByteBuf Class Documentation

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/utils/FriendlyByteBuf

Detailed documentation for the FriendlyByteBuf class, including its constructor and all methods, inherited from io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf.

```APIDOC
## Class: FriendlyByteBuf

### Description
A custom implementation of `io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf` used within the MythicCraft IO project. It extends Netty's `ByteBuf` to provide additional functionality or integration specific to MythicCraft.

### Hierarchy
- `java.lang.Object`
- `io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf`
- `io.lumine.mythic.core.utils.FriendlyByteBuf`

### Implemented Interfaces
- `io.netty.util.ReferenceCounted`
- `Comparable<io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf>`

## Constructor Summary

### `FriendlyByteBuf(io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf bytebuf)`
- **Description**: Constructs a new `FriendlyByteBuf` instance, wrapping an existing `io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf`.
- **Parameters**:
  - `bytebuf` (io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf) - The `ByteBuf` to wrap.

## Method Summary

All methods are inherited from `io.netty.buffer.ByteBuf` unless otherwise specified.

### ByteBufAllocator Methods
- `alloc()`: Returns the `ByteBufAllocator` that created this buffer.

### Array Access Methods
- `array()`: Returns the underlying byte array if this buffer is backed by an array.
- `arrayOffset()`: Returns the offset of the underlying byte array.
- `hasArray()`: Checks if this buffer is backed by an array.

### Capacity and Index Methods
- `capacity()`: Returns the total capacity of this buffer.
- `capacity(int i)`: Sets the capacity of this buffer.
- `discardReadBytes()`: Discards all acknowledged read bytes from this buffer.
- `discardSomeReadBytes()`: Discards some acknowledged read bytes from this buffer.
- `ensureWritable(int i)`: Ensures that the buffer has at least the specified number of writable bytes.
- `ensureWritable(int i, boolean flag)`: Ensures that the buffer has at least the specified number of writable bytes, with an option to release the buffer if allocation fails.
- `maxCapacity()`: Returns the maximum capacity of this buffer.
- `maxWritableBytes()`: Returns the maximum number of bytes that can be written to this buffer without resizing.
- `readerIndex()`: Returns the current reader index of this buffer.
- `readerIndex(int i)`: Sets the reader index of this buffer.
- `writerIndex()`: Returns the current writer index of this buffer.
- `writerIndex(int i)`: Sets the writer index of this buffer.
- `markReaderIndex()`: Marks the current reader index.
- `markWriterIndex()`: Marks the current writer index.
- `readableBytes()`: Returns the number of readable bytes in this buffer.
- `writableBytes()`: Returns the number of writable bytes in this buffer.

### Byte Access Methods
- `getByte(int i)`: Gets a byte at the specified index.
- `setByte(int i, int value)`: Sets a byte at the specified index.
- `readByte()`: Reads a byte from the current reader index and advances the reader index.
- `writeByte(int value)`: Writes a byte to the current writer index and advances the writer index.

### Boolean Access Methods
- `getBoolean(int i)`: Gets a boolean value at the specified index.
- `readBoolean()`: Reads a boolean value from the current reader index and advances the reader index.
- `writeBoolean(boolean value)`: Writes a boolean value to the current writer index and advances the writer index.

### Bytes Access Methods
- `getBytes(int i, byte[])…`: Reads bytes from the specified index into a byte array.
- `readBytes(byte[]…)`: Reads bytes from the current reader index into a byte array.
- `writeBytes(byte[]…)`: Writes bytes from a byte array to the current writer index.
- `getBytes(int i, ByteBuf…)`: Reads bytes from the specified index into another `ByteBuf`.
- `readBytes(ByteBuf…)`: Reads bytes from the current reader index into another `ByteBuf`.
- `writeBytes(ByteBuf…)`: Writes bytes from another `ByteBuf` to the current writer index.
- `readBytes(int i)`: Reads a specified number of bytes from the current reader index.
- `getBytes(int i, OutputStream…)`: Reads bytes from the specified index into an `OutputStream`.
- `readBytes(OutputStream…)`: Reads bytes from the current reader index into an `OutputStream`.
- `writeBytes(OutputStream…)`: Writes bytes from an `OutputStream` to the current writer index.
- `getBytes(int i, ByteBuffer…)`: Reads bytes from the specified index into a `ByteBuffer`.
- `readBytes(ByteBuffer…)`: Reads bytes from the current reader index into a `ByteBuffer`.
- `writeBytes(ByteBuffer…)`: Writes bytes from a `ByteBuffer` to the current writer index.
- `getBytes(int i, FileChannel…)`: Reads bytes from the specified index into a `FileChannel`.
- `readBytes(FileChannel…)`: Reads bytes from the current reader index into a `FileChannel`.
- `writeBytes(FileChannel…)`: Writes bytes from a `FileChannel` to the current writer index.
- `getBytes(int i, GatheringByteChannel…)`: Reads bytes from the specified index into a `GatheringByteChannel`.
- `readBytes(GatheringByteChannel…)`: Reads bytes from the current reader index into a `GatheringByteChannel`.
- `writeBytes(GatheringByteChannel…)`: Writes bytes from a `GatheringByteChannel` to the current writer index.

### Primitive Type Access Methods
- `getChar(int i)`, `readChar()`, `writeChar(int value)`: Accesses `char` values.
- `getDouble(int i)`, `readDouble()`, `writeDouble(double value)`: Accesses `double` values.
- `getFloat(int i)`, `readFloat()`, `writeFloat(float value)`: Accesses `float` values.
- `getInt(int i)`, `readInt()`, `writeInt(int value)`: Accesses `int` values.
- `getIntLE(int i)`, `readIntLE()`, `writeIntLE(int value)`: Accesses little-endian `int` values.
- `getLong(int i)`, `readLong()`, `writeLong(long value)`: Accesses `long` values.
- `getLongLE(int i)`, `readLongLE()`, `writeLongLE(long value)`: Accesses little-endian `long` values.
- `getMedium(int i)`, `readMedium()`, `writeMedium(int value)`: Accesses 3-byte signed integer values.
- `getMediumLE(int i)`, `readMediumLE()`, `writeMediumLE(int value)`: Accesses little-endian 3-byte signed integer values.
- `getShort(int i)`, `readShort()`, `writeShort(int value)`: Accesses `short` values.
- `getShortLE(int i)`, `readShortLE()`, `writeShortLE(int value)`: Accesses little-endian `short` values.

### Unsigned Primitive Type Access Methods
- `getUnsignedByte(int i)`, `readUnsignedByte()`, `writeUnsignedByte(int value)`: Accesses unsigned byte values.
- `getUnsignedInt(int i)`, `readUnsignedInt()`, `writeUnsignedInt(long value)`: Accesses unsigned `int` values.
- `getUnsignedIntLE(int i)`, `readUnsignedIntLE()`, `writeUnsignedIntLE(long value)`: Accesses little-endian unsigned `int` values.
- `getUnsignedMedium(int i)`, `readUnsignedMedium()`, `writeUnsignedMedium(int value)`: Accesses unsigned 3-byte integer values.
- `getUnsignedMediumLE(int i)`, `readUnsignedMediumLE()`, `writeUnsignedMediumLE(int value)`: Accesses little-endian unsigned 3-byte integer values.
- `getUnsignedShort(int i)`, `readUnsignedShort()`, `writeUnsignedShort(int value)`: Accesses unsigned `short` values.
- `getUnsignedShortLE(int i)`, `readUnsignedShortLE()`, `writeUnsignedShortLE(int value)`: Accesses little-endian unsigned `short` values.

### Other Methods
- `asReadOnly()`: Returns a read-only view of this buffer.
- `bytesBefore(byte b0)`: Returns the number of bytes before the first occurrence of the specified byte.
- `bytesBefore(int i, byte b0)`: Returns the number of bytes before the first occurrence of the specified byte, starting from the specified index.
- `bytesBefore(int i, int j, byte b0)`: Returns the number of bytes before the first occurrence of the specified byte within a specified range.
- `clear()`: Clears this buffer by setting the reader and writer indices to 0.
- `compareTo(ByteBuf bytebuf)`: Compares this buffer with another `ByteBuf`.
- `copy()`: Returns a copy of this buffer.

## Interface: PlaceholderDouble

### Description
Represents a placeholder that provides a double value. It extends the `IPlaceholder` interface and offers methods to retrieve the double value with different contextual parameters.

- `StaticDouble`

## Method Summary

  - Description: Creates a `PlaceholderDouble` instance from a string representation.
  - Method: `static`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Static method)

### Instance Methods

- **`double get()`**
  - Description: Retrieves the double value without any specific context.
  - Method: `instance`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Instance method)

- **`double get(PlaceholderMeta meta)`**
  - Description: Retrieves the double value with `PlaceholderMeta` context.
  - Method: `instance`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Instance method)

- **`double get(AbstractEntity entity)`**
  - Description: Retrieves the double value with `AbstractEntity` context.
  - Method: `instance`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Instance method)

- **`double get(PlaceholderMeta meta, AbstractEntity entity)`**
  - Description: Retrieves the double value with both `PlaceholderMeta` and `AbstractEntity` context.
  - Method: `instance`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Instance method)

- **`double get(SkillCaster caster)`**
  - Description: Retrieves the double value with `SkillCaster` context.
  - Method: `instance`
  - Endpoint: N/A (Instance method)

- **`boolean isStaticallyEqualTo(double value)`**
  - Description: Checks if the placeholder's static value is equal to the provided double value.

  - Endpoint: N/A (Instance method)

## AbstractLocation Coordinate Methods

### Description
Methods for accessing and modifying the X, Y, and Z coordinates, as well as block and chunk coordinates.

### Entity Velocity API

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/adapters/VirtualEntity

Manage the velocity of an entity, allowing you to get and set its movement vector.

```APIDOC
## GET /entities/{id}/velocity

### Description
Retrieves the current velocity vector of the entity.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
/entities/{id}/velocity

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **id** (string) - Required - The unique identifier of the entity.

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **velocity** (object) - The velocity vector (e.g., {x: 0.0, y: 0.0, z: 0.0}).

#### Response Example
{
  "velocity": {
    "x": 0.5,
    "y": 0.0,
    "z": -0.1
  }
}

## PUT /entities/{id}/velocity

### Description
Sets the velocity vector of the entity.

### Method
PUT

### Endpoint
/entities/{id}/velocity

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **id** (string) - Required - The unique identifier of the entity.

#### Request Body
- **velocity** (object) - Required - The new velocity vector (e.g., {x: 0.0, y: 0.0, z: 0.0}).

### Request Example
{
  "velocity": {
    "x": 0.0,
    "y": 1.0,
    "z": 0.0
  }
}

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **success** (boolean) - Indicates if the velocity was set successfully.

#### Response Example
{
  "success": true
}
```

### io.lumine.mythic.api.adapters

This section details the abstract classes within the io.lumine.mythic.api.adapters package, which serve as foundational elements for adapter implementations in MythicCraft.

```APIDOC
## Abstract Classes in io.lumine.mythic.api.adapters

### Description
These are the core abstract classes provided by the MythicCraft API for adapters. They define common structures and behaviors for game elements.

### Classes
- **AbstractBiome**: Represents an abstract biome.
- **AbstractBlock**: Represents an abstract block.
- **AbstractEntity**: Represents an abstract entity.
- **AbstractFirework**: Represents an abstract firework.
- **AbstractGameMode**: Represents an abstract game mode.
- **AbstractItemStack**: Represents an abstract item stack.
- **AbstractLocation**: Represents an abstract location.
- **AbstractPlayer**: Represents an abstract player.
- **AbstractSkillAdapter**: Represents an abstract skill adapter.
- **AbstractTaskManager**: Represents an abstract task manager.
- **AbstractVector**: Represents a mutable vector.
- **AbstractWorld**: Represents an abstract world.
```

### MMOItemsSupport Class

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/compatibility/MMOItemsSupport

This section details the MMOItemsSupport class, its constructors, and its methods for interacting with MMOItems functionalities.

```APIDOC
## Class MMOItemsSupport

### Description
Provides support for integrating MMOItems functionalities within MythicBukkit. This class extends `ReloadableModule` and implements several `Terminable` interfaces.

### Fields Inherited
- `autoload`, `plugin`, `terminableRegistry` (from `io.lumine.utils.plugin.PluginModule`)
- `EMPTY` (from `io.lumine.utils.terminable.Terminable`)

### Nested Classes
- `MMOItemsSupport.MMOItemsBlock`
- `MMOItemsSupport.MMOItemsDrop`

### Constructor
#### `MMOItemsSupport(MythicBukkit plugin)`

**Description**: Constructs a new `MMOItemsSupport` instance.

### Methods
#### `load(MythicBukkit plugin)`

**Description**: Loads the MMOItems support module. Overrides the `load` method from `PluginModule`.

**Method**: `void`

#### `unload()`

**Description**: Unloads the MMOItems support module. Overrides the `unload` method from `PluginModule`.

**Method**: `void`

#### `checkCustomBlock(org.bukkit.block.Block block, int id)`

**Description**: Checks if a given block is a custom MMOItems block with the specified ID.

**Method**: `boolean`

**Parameters**:
- **block** (org.bukkit.block.Block) - The block to check.
- **id** (int) - The custom block ID.

#### `getCustomBlockMaterial(int id)`

**Description**: Retrieves the `Material` of a custom block by its ID.

**Method**: `org.bukkit.Material`

**Parameters**:
- **id** (int) - The custom block ID.

#### `getCustomBlockState(int id)`

**Description**: Retrieves the state of a custom block by its ID.

**Method**: `net.Indyuce.mmoitems.api.util.MushroomState`

**Parameters**:
- **id** (int) - The custom block ID.

#### `getMMOItemsBlock(int id)`

**Description**: Retrieves the `AbstractBlock` representation of a custom block by its ID.

**Method**: `AbstractBlock`

**Parameters**:
- **id** (int) - The custom block ID.

#### `getCustomItem(String string)`

**Description**: Retrieves an `ItemStack` representation of a custom item by its string identifier.

**Method**: `org.bukkit.inventory.ItemStack`

**Parameters**:
- **string** (String) - The item's string identifier.

#### `getCustomMMOBlock(int id)`

**Description**: Retrieves the `AbstractBlock` representation of a custom MMOItems block by its ID.

**Method**: `AbstractBlock`

**Parameters**:
- **id** (int) - The custom block ID.

### Methods Inherited from `ReloadableModule`
- `reload`

### Methods Inherited from `PluginModule`
- `bind`, `close`, `doBinding`, `getPlugin`, `setup`

### Methods Inherited from `Terminable`

- `bindModule`

- `bindModuleWith`

- **Parameters**:

  - `reason` (SpawnReason): The reason for spawning the entity.
  - `prespawnFunc` (Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>): A function to execute before the entity is fully spawned.

#### `applyOptions(Entity e)`
- **Description**: Applies various options to an existing Bukkit entity, likely for integration with MythicCraft functionalities.
- **Method**: `org.bukkit.entity.Entity`
- **Inheritance**: Specified by `applyOptions` in class `BukkitEntityType`.
- **Parameters**:
  - `e` (org.bukkit.entity.Entity): The Bukkit entity to apply options to.

## BukkitZoglin Methods

### Method: spawn
#### Description
Spawns a BukkitZoglin entity at the specified location with optional pre-spawn logic.

#### Method
`org.bukkit.entity.Entity spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`

#### Parameters
- **location** (org.bukkit.Location) - The location where the entity will spawn.
- **reason** (SpawnReason) - The reason for spawning the entity.
- **prespawnFunc** (Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>) - A function to execute before the entity is fully spawned.

#### Request Example
```java
BukkitZoglin zoglin = new BukkitZoglin();
Location loc = ...; // Define spawn location
SpawnReason reason = SpawnReason.CUSTOM;
Consumer<Entity> preSpawn = entity -> {
    // Customize entity before spawning
};
zoglin.spawn(loc, reason, preSpawn);
```

### Method: applyOptions

```

### Method: compare

- **e** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The entity to compare with.

#### Request Example

### Get Biome at Location (Bukkit)

import io.lumine.mythic.bukkit.adapters.BukkitWorld;
import io.lumine.mythic.bukkit.adapters.AbstractLocation;
import io.lumine.mythic.bukkit.adapters.AbstractBiome;

// Assuming 'world' is a BukkitWorld and 'location' is an AbstractLocation

### Method: spawn(Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<Entity> prespawnFunc)

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/entities/BukkitBlockDisplay

Spawns a BukkitBlockDisplay entity at a specific location with optional pre-spawn configuration.

```APIDOC
## Method: spawn(Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<Entity> prespawnFunc)

### Description
Spawns the `BukkitBlockDisplay` entity in the game world at the specified `location`. The `reason` parameter indicates why the entity is being spawned, and `prespawnFunc` allows for custom modifications to the entity before it fully spawns.

### Method
`public org.bukkit.entity.Entity spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`

### Endpoint
N/A (Instance method)

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
None

#### Query Parameters
None

#### Request Body
- **location** (org.bukkit.Location) - Required - The world location where the entity will be spawned.
- **reason** (SpawnReason) - Required - The reason for spawning the entity (e.g., CUSTOM, SPAWNER_EGG).
- **prespawnFunc** (Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>) - Optional - A function to execute on the entity just before it is spawned.

### Request Example
```java
Location loc = new Location(world, x, y, z);
BukkitBlockDisplay display = new BukkitBlockDisplay();

// Example prespawn function to set custom name
Consumer<Entity> customName = entity -> entity.setCustomName("My Block Display");

Entity spawnedEntity = display.spawn(loc, SpawnReason.CUSTOM, customName);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **spawnedEntity** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The spawned entity instance.

#### Response Example
(Returns an instance of `org.bukkit.entity.Entity`)

### Error Handling
May return null or throw exceptions if the location is invalid or other spawning conflicts occur.
```

```APIDOC
## BukkitPillager Class

### Description
Represents a Pillager entity within the MythicCraft Bukkit environment, extending `BukkitEntityType`.

### Method Summary

#### Constructors
- `BukkitPillager()`: Constructs a new BukkitPillager.

#### Instance Methods
- `applyOptions(org.bukkit.entity.Entity entity)`: Applies entity-specific options.
- `compare(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e)`: Compares this BukkitPillager with another entity.
- `getHeight()`: Returns the height of the Pillager.
- `instantiate(MythicConfig mc)`: Instantiates the Pillager with MythicConfig.
- `spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`: Spawns a Pillager at a given location with specified parameters.

#### Static Methods
- `isInstanceOf(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e)`: Checks if an entity is an instance of BukkitPillager.

### Method Details

#### `instantiate(MythicConfig mc)`

* **Description**: Instantiates the Pillager with MythicConfig.
* **Method**: `void`
* **Endpoint**: N/A (Class method)
* **Parameters**: 
  * `mc` (MythicConfig) - The MythicConfig object.
* **Inheritance**: Specified by `instantiate` in class `MythicEntity`.

#### `spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`

* **Description**: Spawns a Pillager at a given location with specified parameters.
* **Method**: `org.bukkit.entity.Entity`
* **Endpoint**: N/A (Class method)
* **Parameters**: 
  * `location` (org.bukkit.Location) - The location to spawn the Pillager.
  * `reason` (SpawnReason) - The reason for spawning.
  * `prespawnFunc` (Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>) - A function to execute before spawning.
* **Inheritance**: Specified by `spawn` in class `BukkitEntityType`.

#### `applyOptions(org.bukkit.entity.Entity entity)`

### AbstractEntity API

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/api/adapters/AbstractEntity

This section details the various methods available for interacting with AbstractEntity objects, including methods for getting and setting health, location, armor, and more.

```APIDOC
## AbstractEntity API

### Description
Provides a comprehensive set of methods for managing and querying entity properties and states within the game.

### Method Summary

All Methods
Instance Methods
Abstract Methods

#### **Entity Properties & State**

- **`getHealth()`** (double) - Returns the current health of the entity.
- **`getMaxHealth()`** (double) - Returns the maximum health of the entity.
- **`setHealth(double d)`** (void) - Sets the current health of the entity.
- **`setHealthAndMax(double d)`** (void) - Sets both the current and maximum health of the entity.
- **`getDamage()`** (double) - Returns the current damage value of the entity.
- **`getDamageBase()`** (double) - Returns the base damage value of the entity.
- **`setDamage(double value)`** (void) - Sets the damage value of the entity.
- **`getArmor()`** (double) - Returns the current armor value of the entity.
- **`getArmorBase()`** (double) - Returns the base armor value of the entity.
- **`setArmor(double armor)`** (void) - Sets the armor value of the entity.
- **`isDead()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is dead.
- **`isValid()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is valid.
- **`remove()`** (boolean) - Removes the entity from the game.

#### **Entity Relationships & Location**

- **`getLocation()`** (AbstractLocation) - Gets the current location of the entity.
- **`teleport(AbstractLocation l)`** (void) - Teleports the entity to a specified location.
- **`getBukkitEntity()`** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - Retrieves the Bukkit entity associated with this abstract entity.
- **`getUniqueId()`** (UUID) - Gets the unique identifier of the entity.
- **`hasLineOfSight(AbstractEntity e)`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity has line of sight to another entity.

#### **Entity Type Checks**

- **`isLiving()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is a living entity.
- **`isMob()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is a mob.
- **`isCreature()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is a creature.
- **`isPlayer()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is a player.
- **`isAnimal()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is an animal.
- **`isWaterMob()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is a water mob.
- **`isFlyingMob()`** (boolean) - Checks if the entity is a flying mob.

#### **Entity Management**

- **`setRemoveWhenFarAway(boolean bool)`** (void) - Sets whether the entity should be removed when far away.
- **`setSaveToDisk(boolean bool)`** (void) - Sets whether the entity should be saved to disk.

### Request Example

This API is primarily used via direct method calls within a Java environment and does not typically involve HTTP request/response bodies in the same way as a REST API. The examples below represent method calls.

```java
// Example: Getting and setting entity health
AbstractEntity entity = ...; // Obtain an entity instance
double currentHealth = entity.getHealth();
System.out.println("Current health: " + currentHealth);
entity.setHealth(10.0);

// Example: Teleporting an entity
AbstractLocation newLocation = ...; // Obtain a new location instance
entity.teleport(newLocation);
```

### Response

Responses are typically the return values of the methods called or void for methods that perform actions.

#### Success Response (Method Return)

- **Method Return Value**: The type and value returned by the specific method called (e.g., `double` for `getHealth()`, `boolean` for `isDead()`, `void` for `remove()`).

#### Response Example

```java
// Example: Checking if an entity is dead
AbstractEntity entity = ...;
boolean isDead = entity.isDead();
if (isDead) {
    System.out.println("Entity is dead.");
}
```
```

--------------------------------

### Server Interface Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/api/adapters/ServerInterface

This section details various methods available through the Server Interface for interacting with the game server.

```APIDOC
## Server Interface API

### Description
This API provides access to server-level functionalities within MythicCraft, allowing developers to manage entities, players, worlds, and other game-related objects.

### Methods

#### Get Worlds
Retrieves a list of all available worlds on the server.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)

#### Dispatch Command
Executes a server command.

- **Method**: POST
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **s** (String) - Required - The command string to execute.

#### Get Online Players
Retrieves a list of all players currently online on the server.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)

#### Get Online Player Count
Retrieves the number of players currently online on the server.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Returns**: Integer representing the player count.

#### New Location (Basic)
Creates a new location object with world coordinates.

- **Method**: POST
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **w** (AbstractWorld) - Required - The world for the location.
  - **x** (double) - Required - The X-coordinate.
  - **y** (double) - Required - The Y-coordinate.
  - **z** (double) - Required - The Z-coordinate.

#### Get World by Name
Retrieves a specific world by its name.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **world** (String) - Required - The name of the world to retrieve.

#### Is Valid Biome
Checks if an object is a valid biome.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **o** (Object) - Required - The object to check.
- **Returns**: Boolean indicating if the object is a valid biome.

#### Create Boss Bar
Creates a new boss bar with specified title, color, and style.

- **Method**: POST
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **title** (String) - Required - The title of the boss bar.
  - **color** (AbstractBossBar.BarColor) - Required - The color of the boss bar.
  - **style** (AbstractBossBar.BarStyle) - Required - The style of the boss bar.

#### Get Player by UUID
Retrieves a player by their unique identifier (UUID).

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **uuid** (UUID) - Required - The UUID of the player.

#### Get Player by Name
Retrieves a player by their username.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **name** (String) - Required - The name of the player.

#### Get Entity by UUID
Retrieves an entity by its unique identifier (UUID).

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **uuid** (UUID) - Required - The UUID of the entity.

#### Get World by UUID
Retrieves a specific world by its unique identifier (UUID).

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **uuid** (UUID) - Required - The UUID of the world.

#### Handle Mob Despawn Event
Handles the event when a mob despawns.

- **Method**: POST
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **mob** (ActiveMob) - Required - The active mob instance.

#### Get Mythic Entity Type
Retrieves the MythicEntityType for a given Bukkit entity.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **e** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - Required - The Bukkit entity.

#### New Location (Advanced)
Creates a new location object with world coordinates, yaw, and pitch.

- **Method**: POST
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **w** (AbstractWorld) - Required - The world for the location.
  - **x** (double) - Required - The X-coordinate.
  - **y** (double) - Required - The Y-coordinate.
  - **z** (double) - Required - The Z-coordinate.
  - **yaw** (float) - Required - The yaw rotation.
  - **pitch** (float) - Required - The pitch rotation.

#### Create Firework
Creates a firework effect.

- **Method**: POST
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **type** (String) - Required - The type of the firework.
  - **duration** (int) - Required - The duration of the firework effect.
  - **colors** (Collection<Color>) - Required - The primary colors of the firework.
  - **fadeColors** (Collection<Color>) - Required - The fade colors of the firework.
  - **flicker** (boolean) - Required - Whether the firework should flicker.
  - **trail** (boolean) - Required - Whether the firework should have a trail.

#### Get Biome by Name
Retrieves a biome by its name.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **biome** (String) - Required - The name of the biome.

#### Get Biomes by Search
Retrieves a collection of biomes matching a search query.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **biomeSearch** (String) - Required - The search query for biomes.

#### Get Block by Name
Retrieves a block by its name.

- **Method**: GET
- **Endpoint**: Not applicable (method call)
- **Parameters**:
  - **name** (String) - Required - The name of the block.

```

### IMultiDrop Interface

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/api/drops/IMultiDrop

This section details the IMultiDrop interface, which defines methods for handling multiple drop types. It includes the 'get' method for retrieving a LootBag.

```APIDOC
## IMultiDrop Interface

### Description
The `IMultiDrop` interface represents a contract for objects that can provide multiple types of drops. It primarily includes a method to retrieve a loot bag based on given metadata.

### Known Implementing Classes
* `DropTableDrop`
* `MythicDropsDrop`
* `PhatLootsDrop`

### Method Summary

#### Instance Methods
* `LootBag get(DropMetadata meta)` - Retrieves a loot bag based on the provided drop metadata.

### Method Details

#### get
`LootBag get(DropMetadata meta)`

##### Description
This method is responsible for generating and returning a `LootBag` object. The content and nature of the loot bag are determined by the `DropMetadata` object passed as an argument, which likely contains information about the context of the drop (e.g., the entity that died, the location, etc.).

##### Parameters

* **meta** (`DropMetadata`) - Required - Metadata associated with the drop, used to determine the loot.

##### Response

* **Success Response (LootBag)**
  * `LootBag` - An object representing the collection of items to be dropped.
```

### Spawn Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/adapters/BukkitEntityType

Provides various methods for spawning entities with different configurations.

```APIDOC
## POST /spawn (Multiple Overloads)

### Description
These methods are responsible for spawning entities. They offer flexibility by allowing different parameters such as `AbstractLocation`, `Location`, `SpawnReason`, and an optional `prespawnFunc`.

### Method
`POST` (Conceptual, as these are Java methods)

### Endpoint
N/A (Java Methods)

### Parameters

#### Method: `spawn(AbstractLocation location, SpawnReason reason)`
- **location** (`AbstractLocation`) - Required - The location where the entity will spawn.
- **reason** (`SpawnReason`) - Required - The reason for the entity spawning.

#### Method: `spawn(AbstractLocation location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`
- **location** (`AbstractLocation`) - Required - The location where the entity will spawn.
- **reason** (`SpawnReason`) - Required - The reason for the entity spawning.
- **prespawnFunc** (`Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>`) - Optional - A function to execute on the entity before it is fully spawned.

#### Method: `spawnBaseEntity(AbstractLocation location, SpawnReason reason)`
- **location** (`AbstractLocation`) - Required - The location where the base entity will spawn.
- **reason** (`SpawnReason`) - Required - The reason for the entity spawning.

#### Method: `spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason)`
- **location** (`org.bukkit.Location`) - Required - The Bukkit location where the entity will spawn.
- **reason** (`SpawnReason`) - Required - The reason for the entity spawning.

#### Method: `spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`
- **location** (`org.bukkit.Location`) - Required - The Bukkit location where the entity will spawn.
- **reason** (`SpawnReason`) - Required - The reason for the entity spawning.
- **prespawnFunc** (`Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>`) - Optional - A function to execute on the entity before it is fully spawned.

#### Method: `spawnEntity(org.bukkit.Location location, org.bukkit.entity.EntityType type, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`
- **location** (`org.bukkit.Location`) - Required - The Bukkit location where the entity will spawn.
- **type** (`org.bukkit.entity.EntityType`) - Required - The type of entity to spawn.
- **reason** (`SpawnReason`) - Required - The reason for the entity spawning.
- **prespawnFunc** (`Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>`) - Optional - A function to execute on the entity before it is fully spawned.

### Request Example
```java
// Example for spawn(AbstractLocation location, SpawnReason reason)
AbstractLocation loc = ...;
SpawnReason reason = SpawnReason.SPAWNER_EGG;
AbstractEntity spawnedEntity = bukkitEntityType.spawn(loc, reason);

// Example for spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)
org.bukkit.Location bukkitLoc = ...;
Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> func = entity -> {
    // Do something with the entity before it spawns
};
bukkitEntityType.spawn(bukkitLoc, reason, func);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **AbstractEntity** or **org.bukkit.entity.Entity** - The spawned entity object.

#### Response Example
```json
// Represents the spawned entity object (specifics depend on the entity type)
{
  "uuid": "...",
  "type": "ZOMBIE",
  "location": { ... }
}
```
```

### Command Helper Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/commands/CommandHelper

This section details the static methods available in the CommandHelper class for interacting with command senders.

```APIDOC
## Command Helper API

### Description
Provides static utility methods for sending various types of messages to command senders in a structured format.

### Methods

#### `sendHeader(CommandSender sender)`

*   **Description**: Sends a predefined header message to the command sender.
*   **Method**: `static void`
*   **Endpoint**: N/A (Local method call)
*   **Parameters**:
    *   **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The recipient of the header message.

#### `sendFooter(CommandSender sender)`

*   **Description**: Sends a predefined footer message to the command sender.
*   **Method**: `static void`
*   **Endpoint**: N/A (Local method call)
*   **Parameters**:
    *   **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The recipient of the footer message.

#### `send(CommandSender sender, String message)`

*   **Description**: Sends a plain string message to the command sender.
*   **Method**: `static void`
*   **Endpoint**: N/A (Local method call)
*   **Parameters**:
    *   **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The recipient of the message.
    *   **message** (String) - Required - The message content to send.

#### `sendSuccess(CommandSender sender, String message)`

*   **Description**: Sends a success message, typically styled differently, to the command sender.
*   **Method**: `static void`
*   **Endpoint**: N/A (Local method call)
*   **Parameters**:
    *   **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The recipient of the success message.
    *   **message** (String) - Required - The success message content.

*   **Description**: Sends an error message, typically styled differently, to the command sender.

*   **Endpoint**: N/A (Local method call)
*   **Parameters**:
    *   **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The recipient of the error message.
    *   **message** (String) - Required - The error message content.

*   **Endpoint**: N/A (Local method call)

### BukkitEntity - Equipment and Interaction

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/adapters/BukkitEntity

APIs for equipping items, interacting with passengers, and handling custom names.

```APIDOC
## BukkitEntity - Equipment and Interaction

### Description
Manage entity equipment, passengers, and custom names.

### Equipment
* **Method**: `equipItemChest(AbstractItemStack item)`
* **Description**: Equips an item to the entity's chest slot.

* **Method**: `equipItemFeet(AbstractItemStack item)`
* **Description**: Equips an item to the entity's feet slot.

* **Method**: `equipItemHead(AbstractItemStack item)`
* **Description**: Equips an item to the entity's head slot.

* **Method**: `equipItemLegs(AbstractItemStack item)`
* **Description**: Equips an item to the entity's legs slot.

* **Method**: `equipItemMainHand(AbstractItemStack item)`
* **Description**: Equips an item to the entity's main hand.

* **Method**: `equipItemOffHand(AbstractItemStack item)`
* **Description**: Equips an item to the entity's off-hand.

### Passengers
* **Method**: `setPassenger(AbstractEntity entity)`
* **Description**: Sets another entity as a passenger of this entity.

* **Method**: `getPassenger()`
* **Returns**: `AbstractEntity`
* **Description**: Gets the entity currently riding this entity.

### Custom Name
* **Method**: `getCustomName()`
* **Returns**: `String`
* **Description**: Gets the custom name of the entity.

* **Method**: `setCustomName(String name)`
* **Description**: Sets a custom name for the entity.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "example": "// Equipping an item\nAbstractItemStack helmet = ...;\nmyEntity.equipItemHead(helmet);\n\n// Setting a custom name\nmyEntity.setCustomName(\"My Pet\");"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
* **Description**: Equipment and interaction methods typically return `void` or the relevant object type (e.g., `AbstractEntity` for `getPassenger`).

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "example": "// Getting the custom name\nString name = myEntity.getCustomName();"
}
```
```

## POST /mythic/item

### Description
Creates a MythicItem instance using a provided Pack, File, internal name, and optional MythicConfig.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/mythic/item`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **pack** (object) - Required - The Pack object to associate with the item.
- **file** (object) - Required - The File object representing the item's configuration.
- **internalName** (string) - Required - The internal name of the item.
- **mc** (object) - Optional - The MythicConfig object for the item.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "pack": {
    "packName": "example_pack"
  },
  "file": {
    "fileName": "sword.yml"
  },
  "internalName": "mythic_sword",
  "mc": {
    "configKey": "configValue"
  }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **MythicItem** (object) - The created MythicItem instance.

#### Response Example

{
  "itemName": "mythic_sword",
  "packName": "example_pack"
}
```

### Entity Metadata Operations

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/adapters/BukkitEntity

Methods for setting, getting, checking, and removing metadata associated with an entity. Metadata allows for custom data storage on entities.

```APIDOC
## POST /entities/{id}/metadata

### Description
Sets a metadata value for the entity.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/entities/{id}/metadata

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **id** (string) - Required - The unique identifier of the entity.

#### Request Body
- **key** (string) - Required - The key for the metadata.
- **value** (object) - Required - The value to associate with the key.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "key": "custom_data",
  "value": {
    "level": 10,
    "status": "active"
  }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Confirmation of metadata being set.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Metadata 'custom_data' set successfully."
}
```

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **id** (string) - Required - The unique identifier of the entity.

#### Query Parameters
- **key** (string) - Required - The key of the metadata to check.

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **exists** (boolean) - True if the metadata key exists, false otherwise.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "exists": true
}
```

---

## DELETE /entities/{id}/metadata/{key}

### Description
Removes the metadata associated with a specific key from the entity.

### Method
DELETE

### Endpoint
/entities/{id}/metadata/{key}

### Parameters
#### Path Parameters
- **id** (string) - Required - The unique identifier of the entity.
- **key** (string) - Required - The key of the metadata to remove.

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **message** (string) - Confirmation of metadata removal.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "message": "Metadata 'custom_data' removed successfully."
}
```
```

### GoToSpawnLocationGoal - Core Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/mobs/ai/goals/GoToSpawnLocationGoal

Provides details on the core methods used to control the behavior of the GoToSpawnLocationGoal.

```APIDOC
## GoToSpawnLocationGoal - Core Methods

### Description
These methods control the lifecycle and execution of the GoToSpawnLocationGoal, determining when it starts, stops, and performs its actions.

### Methods

#### `shouldStart()`

##### Description
Checks if the AI goal should begin execution.

##### Method
`boolean shouldStart()`

##### Endpoint
N/A

#### `start()`

##### Description
Initializes and begins the execution of the AI goal.

##### Method
`void start()`

##### Endpoint
N/A

#### `tick()`

##### Description
Called every game tick while the AI goal is active, performing its logic.

##### Method
`void tick()`

##### Endpoint
N/A

#### `shouldEnd()`

##### Description
Determines if the AI goal has completed its task and should terminate.

##### Method
`boolean shouldEnd()`

##### Endpoint
N/A

#### `end()`

##### Description
Cleans up resources and stops the execution of the AI goal.

##### Method
`void end()`

##### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
None for these methods.

### Request Example
```java
// Example usage within the AI system
if (goal.shouldStart()) {
    goal.start();
}

while (goal.isActive()) {
    goal.tick();
    if (goal.shouldEnd()) {
        goal.end();
        break;
    }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (Method Execution)
- `shouldStart()`: `boolean` - Returns `true` if the goal should start, `false` otherwise.
- `start()`: `void` - No return value.
- `tick()`: `void` - No return value.
- `shouldEnd()`: `boolean` - Returns `true` if the goal should end, `false` otherwise.
- `end()`: `void` - No return value.

#### Response Example
N/A
```

### BukkitSheep Class Overview

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/entities/BukkitSheep

Provides an overview of the BukkitSheep class, including its fields, constructors, and methods inherited from its superclasses.

```APIDOC
## Class: BukkitSheep

### Description
Represents a sheep entity within the MythicCraft Bukkit API. This class extends `BukkitEntityType` and provides specific implementations for sheep-related functionalities.

### Fields

*   **`color`** (`protected org.bukkit.DyeColor`)
    *   Stores the dye color of the sheep.
*   **`height`** (`protected static final int`)
    *   Represents the standard height of a sheep entity.

### Constructor

*   **`BukkitSheep()`**
    *   Default constructor for creating a `BukkitSheep` instance.

### Methods

*   **`applyOptions(org.bukkit.entity.Entity entity)`**
    *   Applies specific options to a given Bukkit entity.
    *   Inherited from `BukkitEntityType`.
*   **`compare(org.bukkit.entity.Entity e)`**
    *   Compares this `BukkitSheep` instance with another Bukkit entity.
    *   Inherited from `BukkitEntityType`.
*   **`getColor()`**
    *   Returns the current dye color of the sheep.
    *   Returns: `org.bukkit.DyeColor` - The sheep's color.
*   **`getHeight()`**
    *   Returns the height of the sheep entity.
    *   Overrides: `getHeight` in class `MythicEntity`.
    *   Returns: `int` - The height of the sheep.
*   **`instantiate(MythicConfig mc)`**
    *   Initializes the sheep entity using MythicCraft configuration.
    *   Specified by: `instantiate` in class `MythicEntity`.
    *   Parameters:
        *   `mc` (`MythicConfig`) - The configuration object.
*   **`spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)`**
    *   Spawns a new sheep entity at the specified location with given parameters.
    *   Specified by: `spawn` in class `BukkitEntityType`.
    *   Parameters:
        *   `location` (`org.bukkit.Location`) - The location to spawn the sheep.
        *   `reason` (`SpawnReason`) - The reason for spawning.
        *   `prespawnFunc` (`Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>`) - A function to execute before spawning.
    *   Returns: `org.bukkit.entity.Entity` - The spawned sheep entity.

### Inherited Methods

*   **From `io.lumine.mythic.bukkit.adapters.BukkitEntityType`:**
    *   `getHead()`
    *   `getHeadString()`
    *   `getMythicEntity()`
    *   `spawn()` (multiple overloads)
    *   `spawnBaseEntity()`
    *   `spawnEntity()`
*   **From `java.lang.Object`:**
    *   `clone()`, `equals()`, `finalize()`, `getClass()`, `hashCode()`, `notify()`, `notifyAll()`, `toString()`, `wait()
```

### spawn Method

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/entities/BukkitSniffer

Spawns an entity at a given location with specified options.

```APIDOC
## org.bukkit.entity.Entity spawn(org.bukkit.Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity> prespawnFunc)

### Description
Spawns an entity at the specified location with the given reason and an optional prespawn function. This method is specified by the BukkitEntityType class.

### Method
`org.bukkit.entity.Entity`

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **location** (org.bukkit.Location) - Required - The location where the entity will spawn.
- **reason** (SpawnReason) - Required - The reason for spawning the entity.
- **prespawnFunc** (Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>) - Optional - A function to execute before the entity is fully spawned.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "location": {
    "world": "world",
    "x": 0.0,
    "y": 64.0,
    "z": 0.0
  },
  "reason": "SPAWN_EGG",
  "prespawnFunc": null 
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **entity** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The spawned Bukkit entity.

#### Response Example
```json

### BrowseCommand API

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/commands/mobs/BrowseCommand

This section details the core methods of the BrowseCommand class, including command execution, tab completion, permission handling, and command identification.

```APIDOC
## GET /commands/browse

### Description
This endpoint provides information about the BrowseCommand, which is used for browsing various features within the MythicCraft plugin. It handles command execution and provides tab completion suggestions.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/commands/browse`

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The sender of the command.
- **args** (String[]) - Required - The arguments passed to the command.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "sender": "player_name",
  "args": ["mob", "list"]
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **result** (boolean) - Indicates if the command was executed successfully.
- **tabCompletion** (List<String>) - A list of possible tab completions for the command arguments.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "result": true,
  "tabCompletion": ["all", "name", "type"]
}
```

## POST /commands/browse/execute

### Description
Executes the BrowseCommand with the provided sender and arguments.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
`/commands/browse/execute`

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The sender of the command.
- **args** (String[]) - Required - The arguments passed to the command.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "sender": "player_name",
  "args": ["mob", "list"]
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **success** (boolean) - True if the command executed successfully, false otherwise.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "success": true
}
```

## GET /commands/browse/tab-complete

### Description
Provides tab completion suggestions for the BrowseCommand based on the current input.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/commands/browse/tab-complete`

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **sender** (CommandSender) - Required - The sender requesting tab completion.
- **args** (String[]) - Required - The arguments typed so far.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "sender": "player_name",
  "args": ["mob"]
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **suggestions** (List<String>) - A list of possible tab completion strings.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "suggestions": ["all", "name", "type"]
}
```

## GET /commands/browse/permission

### Description
Retrieves the permission node required to execute the BrowseCommand.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/commands/browse/permission`

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **permission** (String) - The permission node string.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "permission": "mythicmobs.browse"
}
```

## GET /commands/browse/name

### Description
Retrieves the primary name of the BrowseCommand.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/commands/browse/name`

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **name** (String) - The command name.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "name": "browse"
}
```

## GET /commands/browse/aliases

### Description
Retrieves all aliases registered for the BrowseCommand.

### Method
GET

### Endpoint
`/commands/browse/aliases`

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **aliases** (String[]) - An array of command aliases.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "aliases": ["b", "br"]
}
```
```
