```APIDOC
## WorldEditSupport.WorldGuardSix Methods

### Description
This section details the instance methods available in the WorldGuardSix class for interacting with WorldGuard version 6 features.

### Methods

#### isLocationInRegions

- **Description**: Checks if a given location is within a specified region.
- **Method**: `public boolean isLocationInRegions(AbstractLocation loc, String region)`
- **Parameters**:
  - `loc` (AbstractLocation) - Required - The location to check.
  - `region` (String) - Required - The name of the region.
- **Response**:
  - `boolean` - True if the location is in the region, false otherwise.
- **Example**:
  ```java
  boolean isInRegion = wg6.isLocationInRegions(myLocation, "my_region");
  ```

- **Description**: Retrieves the flag registry for WorldGuard.

  - `Object` - The flag registry.

  ```

#### getApplicableRegions

- **Method**: `public Object getApplicableRegions(org.bukkit.World world, org.bukkit.util.Vector vector)`
- **Parameters**:

  - `vector` (org.bukkit.util.Vector) - Required - The vector representing the location.

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/skills/targeters/VanillaTargeter

```APIDOC
## Class: VanillaTargeter

### Description
A class that extends `IEntitySelector` and implements `IEntityTargeter` and `ISkillTargeter` for selecting entities within the MythicCraft framework.

### Fields inherited from class io.lumine.mythic.core.skills.targeters.IEntitySelector
- **useBoundingBox** (boolean) - Indicates if bounding box should be used for selection.

### Fields inherited from class io.lumine.mythic.core.skills.SkillTargeter
- **config** (MythicLineConfig) - The configuration object for the skill.
- **parent** (SkillExecutor) - The parent executor of the skill.
- **sudoOwner** (Object) - The sudo owner of the skill.
- **sudoParent** (SkillExecutor) - The sudo parent executor of the skill.
- **sudoTrigger** (Object) - The sudo trigger of the skill.
- **targetConditions** (List<TargetCondition>?) - Conditions to filter targets.
- **targetConditionString** (String) - The string representation of target conditions.

```APIDOC
## Interface: ILocationSelector

### Description
An interface used to define strategies for selecting target locations within the MythicCraft IO system, particularly for skills and targeting mechanisms.

N/A (Interface)

### Endpoint
N/A (Interface)

### Parameters
N/A (Interface)

### Request Example
N/A (Interface)

### Response
N/A (Interface)

---

## Classes Implementing ILocationSelector

### Description
The following classes implement the `ILocationSelector` interface, providing specific logic for determining target locations based on various criteria.

### Method
N/A (List of classes)

### Endpoint
N/A (List of classes)

### Parameters
N/A (List of classes)

### Request Example
N/A (List of classes)

### Response
N/A (List of classes)

#### Subclasses in `io.lumine.mythic.core.skills.targeters`:

- **BlocksInChunkTargeter**
  - Description: Targets blocks within a specific chunk.
- **BlocksInRadiusTargeter**
  - Description: Targets blocks within a specified radius from a point.
- **BlocksNearOriginTargeter**
  - Description: Targets blocks near the origin point.
- **BlockVeinTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a vein of connected blocks.
- **CasterSpawnLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets the spawn location of the caster.
- **ConeTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations in a conical area.
- **FloorOfTargetsTargeter**
  - Description: Targets the floor area relative to other targets.
- **ForwardTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations in the forward direction from the caster.
- **ForwardWallTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations on walls in the forward direction.
- **LineTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations along a line.
- **LocationsOfTargetsTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations based on existing targets.
- **LocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a specific, predefined location.
- **NearestStructureTargeter**
  - Description: Targets the nearest structure.
- **ObstructingBlockTargeter**
  - Description: Targets blocks that obstruct a line of sight.
- **OriginTargeter**
  - Description: Targets the origin point.
- **PlayerLocationsInRadiusTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations of players within a radius.
- **ProjectileForwardTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations in the direction of a projectile.
- **RandomLocationsNearOriginTargeter**
  - Description: Targets random locations near the origin.
- **RandomLocationsNearTargetsTargeter**
  - Description: Targets random locations near other targets.
- **RandomLocationsTargeter**
  - Description: Targets random locations.
- **RandomRingPointTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a random point on a ring.
- **RectangleTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations within a rectangular area.
- **RingAroundOriginTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations in a ring around the origin.
- **RingTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations in a ring.
- **SelfEyeLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets the eye location of the caster.
- **SelfLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets the caster's own location.
- **SpawnersTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations of spawners.
- **SpawnLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a general spawn location.
- **SphereTargeter**
  - Description: Targets locations within a spherical area.
- **TargetBlockTargeter**
  - Description: Targets the block the caster is looking at.
- **TargetedLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a location that has been specifically targeted.
- **TargetLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a general target location.
- **ThreatTableRandomLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a random location based on threat tables.
- **TrackedLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a location that is being tracked.
- **TriggerLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets the location where the skill was triggered.
- **VariableLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a location determined by a variable.
- **WrappedLocationTargeter**
  - Description: Targets a location wrapped by another targeter.
```

### BukkitEndermite Methods

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/entities/BukkitEndermite

Details the methods available for interacting with BukkitEndermite instances, including spawning and entity manipulation.

```APIDOC
## instantiate(MythicConfig mc)

### Description
Instantiates the BukkitEndermite using provided MythicConfig settings.

### Method
`void`

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **mc** (MythicConfig) - Required - Configuration object for instantiation.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "mc": { ... MythicConfig object ... }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
This method does not return a value directly, but modifies the internal state for instantiation.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "status": "instantiated"
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## spawn(Location location, SpawnReason reason, Consumer<Entity> prespawnFunc)

### Description
Spawns a BukkitEndermite at the specified location with given spawn reason and an optional prespawn function.

### Method
`org.bukkit.entity.Entity`

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **location** (Location) - Required - The location at which to spawn the endermite.
- **reason** (SpawnReason) - Required - The reason for spawning the entity.
- **prespawnFunc** (Consumer<Entity>) - Optional - A function to execute before the entity is fully spawned.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "location": {"x": 0.0, "y": 64.0, "z": 0.0, "world": "world"},
  "reason": "CUSTOM",
  "prespawnFunc": null
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
Returns the spawned Bukkit Entity object.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "entityType": "ENDERMITE",
  "location": {"x": 0.0, "y": 64.0, "z": 0.0, "world": "world"}
}
```
```

```APIDOC
## applyOptions(Entity entity)

### Description
Applies entity-specific options to an already spawned Bukkit Entity.

### Method
`org.bukkit.entity.Entity`

### Endpoint
N/A

### Parameters
#### Request Body
- **entity** (Entity) - Required - The Bukkit Entity to apply options to.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "entity": { ... Bukkit Entity object ... }
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
Returns the modified Bukkit Entity object.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "entityType": "ENDERMITE",
  "optionsApplied": true
}
```
```

### Parameters

  "e": { ... Bukkit Entity object ... }
}
```

### Response

### Config Creation API

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/config/ConfigExecutor

Provides methods for creating different types of configurations, including general configurations with file or file configuration objects, and line-specific configurations.

```APIDOC
## POST /mythic/config

### Description
Creates a new Mythic configuration. This endpoint supports creating configurations from a node and a file configuration, or from a node and a file, or from a node, a file, and a file configuration.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/mythic/config

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **node** (String) - Required - The node identifier for the configuration.
- **file** (File) - Optional - The file to associate with the configuration.
- **fileConfiguration** (io.lumine.utils.config.file.FileConfiguration) - Optional - The file configuration object.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "node": "example_node",
  "file": "path/to/your/file.yml",
  "fileConfiguration": { ... } 
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **mythicConfig** (MythicConfig) - The created Mythic configuration object.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "mythicConfig": { ... } 
}
```

## POST /mythic/lineConfig

### Description
Creates a new Mythic line configuration. This endpoint supports creating line configurations from a file and a specific line, or directly from a line string.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/mythic/lineConfig

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **file** (File) - Optional - The file containing the line configuration.
- **line** (String) - Required - The specific line content for the configuration.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "file": "path/to/your/file.txt",
  "line": "This is a specific line of text."
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **mythicLineConfig** (MythicLineConfig) - The created Mythic line configuration object.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "mythicLineConfig": { ... } 
}
```
```

### MythicConditionLoadEvent - Get Handler List (Static)

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/events/MythicConditionLoadEvent

### RandomSpawnPoint Class Overview

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/spawning/random/RandomSpawnPoint

Provides an overview of the RandomSpawnPoint class, including its fields, constructors, and methods.

```APIDOC
## Class: RandomSpawnPoint

### Description
Represents a random spawn point for entities, holding information about the entity, its location, spawn reason, and type.

### Fields
#### Field Summary
- **entity** (AbstractEntity) - Protected field representing the entity to be spawned.
- **levelMod** (float) - Protected field for level modification.
- **location** (AbstractLocation) - Protected final field representing the spawn location.
- **pointType** (SpawnPointType) - Protected final field representing the type of spawn point.
- **spawnReason** (org.bukkit.event.entity.CreatureSpawnEvent.SpawnReason) - Protected field indicating the reason for spawning.

### Constructors
#### Constructor Summary
- **RandomSpawnPoint(AbstractEntity entity, AbstractLocation location, org.bukkit.event.entity.CreatureSpawnEvent.SpawnReason spawnReason)**: Creates a new RandomSpawnPoint with entity, location, and spawn reason.
- **RandomSpawnPoint(AbstractEntity entity, AbstractLocation location, org.bukkit.event.entity.CreatureSpawnEvent.SpawnReason spawnReason, SpawnPointType pointType)**: Creates a new RandomSpawnPoint with entity, location, spawn reason, and spawn point type.
- **RandomSpawnPoint(AbstractLocation location)**: Creates a new RandomSpawnPoint with a location.
- **RandomSpawnPoint(AbstractLocation location, SpawnPointType pointType)**: Creates a new RandomSpawnPoint with a location and spawn point type.

### Methods
#### Method Summary
- **getBiome()** (AbstractBiome): Returns the biome of the spawn point.
- **getReason()** (org.bukkit.event.entity.CreatureSpawnEvent.SpawnReason): Returns the spawn reason.
- **getWorld()** (AbstractWorld): Returns the world of the spawn point.

### Inherited Methods from `java.lang.Object`
`clone, equals, finalize, getClass, hashCode, notify, notifyAll, toString, wait, wait, wait`
```

### Get Entities Near Location (Bukkit)

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/api/adapters/class-use/AbstractLocation

Retrieves a collection of entities within a specified radius of an AbstractLocation. Supports filtering entities with a Predicate.

```java
import io.lumine.mythic.bukkit.adapters.BukkitWorld;
import io.lumine.mythic.bukkit.adapters.AbstractLocation;
import io.lumine.mythic.bukkit.adapters.AbstractEntity;
import java.util.Collection;
import java.util.function.Predicate;

// Assuming 'world' is a BukkitWorld, 'location' is an AbstractLocation
Collection<AbstractEntity> entities = world.getEntitiesNearLocation(location, 50.0);

// With predicate
Predicate<AbstractEntity> filter = entity -> entity.isLiving();
Collection<AbstractEntity> livingEntities = world.getEntitiesNearLocation(location, 50.0, filter);
```

### MenuCommand API

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/commands/menus/MenuCommand

Details about the MenuCommand class, including its constructors and methods for handling commands and tab completions within the MythicBukkit plugin.

```APIDOC
## Class: MenuCommand

### Description
Represents a command within the MythicBukkit plugin, specifically designed for menu interactions. It implements `CommandExecutor` and `TabExecutor` for handling in-game commands and providing tab-completion suggestions.

### Methods

#### `onCommand(CommandSender sender, String[] args)`

*   **Description**: Executes the command logic when a player or console enters the command.
*   **Method**: `boolean`
*   **Parameters**:
    *   `sender` (CommandSender) - The entity that executed the command.
    *   `args` (String[]) - An array of strings representing the arguments passed to the command.
*   **Returns**: `true` if the command was handled successfully, `false` otherwise.

#### `onTabComplete(CommandSender sender, String[] args)`

*   **Description**: Provides suggestions for command arguments as the user types.
*   **Method**: `List<String>`
*   **Parameters**:
    *   `sender` (CommandSender) - The entity requesting tab completion.
    *   `args` (String[]) - An array of strings representing the arguments entered so far.
*   **Returns**: A list of possible string completions for the current argument.

#### `getName()`

*   **Description**: Retrieves the name of the command.
*   **Method**: `String`

#### `getPermissionNode()`

*   **Description**: Gets the permission node required to execute this command.
*   **Method**: `String`

#### `isConsoleFriendly()`

*   **Description**: Checks if the command can be executed from the console.
*   **Method**: `boolean`

### Constructors

#### `MenuCommand(MythicBukkit parent)`

*   **Description**: Constructs a new `MenuCommand` instance with the parent plugin.
*   **Parameters**:
    *   `parent` (MythicBukkit) - The parent MythicBukkit plugin instance.

#### `MenuCommand(Command<MythicBukkit> parent)`

*   **Description**: Constructs a new `MenuCommand` instance with a generic parent command.
*   **Parameters**:
    *   `parent` (Command<MythicBukkit>) - The parent command instance.
```

### MythicMob Spawning API

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/core/mobs/MobType

Provides methods for spawning MythicMobs with various configurations.

```APIDOC
## POST /websites/mythiccraft_io_javadocs_mythic/spawn

### Description
This section details the various methods available for spawning MythicMobs.

### Method
POST

### Endpoint
/websites/mythiccraft_io_javadocs_mythic/spawn

### Parameters
#### Query Parameters
- **location** (AbstractLocation) - Required - The location where the mob will spawn.
- **level** (double) - Required - The level of the spawned mob.
- **reason** (SpawnReason) - Optional - The reason for spawning the mob.
- **prespawnFunc** (Consumer<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>) - Optional - A function to execute before the mob is fully spawned.
- **spawner** (MythicSpawner) - Optional - The spawner responsible for this mob.

### Request Example
```json
{
  "location": "world,x,y,z",
  "level": 1.0,
  "reason": "SPAWNER",
  "prespawnFunc": "// Java code for prespawn function",
  "spawner": "MythicSpawnerInstance"
}
```

### Response
#### Success Response (200)
- **ActiveMob** (ActiveMob) - The spawned active mob instance.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "activeMob": "ActiveMobInstanceDetails"
}
```
```

### MythicMobSpawnEvent - Event Details

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/events/MythicMobSpawnEvent

This section details the MythicMobSpawnEvent, which is called when a MythicMob spawns. It provides methods to get information about the spawned mob, its level, location, and the reason for spawning. It also implements the Cancellable interface, allowing the spawn event to be cancelled.

```APIDOC
## MythicMobSpawnEvent

### Description
Called when a MythicMob spawns. This event allows for modification of mob properties or cancellation of the spawn.

### Method
Event

### Parameters
This event does not have direct parameters in its instantiation that are typically accessed via API calls, but it provides accessors for mob details.

### Request Example
```java
// Example of how to listen for and potentially modify the event
Bukkit.getPluginManager().registerEvents(new Listener() {
    @EventHandler
    public void onMythicMobSpawn(MythicMobSpawnEvent event) {
        // Get mob type, level, and location
        MythicMob mobType = event.getMobType();
        double mobLevel = event.getMobLevel();
        Location loc = event.getLocation();

        // Cancel the spawn if the mob is a specific type or meets certain criteria
        if (mobType.getInternalName().equals("MySpecialMob")) {
            event.setCancelled(true);
        }

        // Set a custom mob level
        event.setMobLevel(mobLevel * 1.5);
    }
}, yourPlugin);
```

### Response
#### Success Response (Event Triggered)
- **getEntity** (`org.bukkit.entity.Entity`) - The Bukkit Entity of the mob being spawned.
- **getLivingEntity** (`org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity`) - Deprecated. The Bukkit LivingEntity of the mob being spawned.
- **getLocation** (`org.bukkit.Location`) - The Bukkit Location of the mob being spawned.
- **getMob** (`ActiveMob`) - The instance of the mob being spawned.
- **getMobLevel** (`double`) - The level of the mob being spawned.
- **getMobType** (`MythicMob`) - The type of the mob being spawned.
- **getMythicSpawner** (`MythicSpawner`) - The internal name of the spawner that spawned this mob.
- **getSpawnReason** (`SpawnReason`) - The reason for the mob spawn.
- **isCancelled** (`boolean`) - Returns true if this event has been cancelled.
- **isFromMythicSpawner** (`Boolean`) - Checks if the mob was spawned from a MythicSpawner.

#### Response Example
```json
{
  "mobType": "Skeleton",
  "mobLevel": 1.0,
  "location": {
    "world": "world",
    "x": 100.0,
    "y": 64.0,
    "z": 200.0
  },
  "spawnReason": "SPAWNER_EGG",
  "isCancelled": false
}
```

#### Error Handling
- **setCancelled(boolean cancel)**: Sets the cancellation state of the event. Setting to `true` will prevent the mob from spawning.
- **setMobLevel(double level)**: Sets the level of the mob being spawned. This can be used to adjust difficulty.
```

### BukkitAPIHelper - Core Functionality

Source: https://www.mythiccraft.io/javadocs/mythic/io/lumine/mythic/bukkit/BukkitAPIHelper

This section covers the primary methods of the BukkitAPIHelper class, including threat manipulation, skill casting, and retrieving MythicMob information.

```APIDOC
## BukkitAPIHelper Class

### Description
A collection of special helper methods to assist developers with basic tasks in MythicMobs. These methods are only usable on Bukkit/Spigot platforms.

Retrieved using `MythicMobs.inst().getAPIHelper();`

**Since:** 4.0.0

**Author:** Ashijin

### Methods

#### `addThreat`

Increases threat for a target in a mob's threat table.

**Method:** N/A (Instance Method)

**Parameters:**
- **mob** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The entity representing the mob.
- **target** (org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity) - The living entity to add threat to.
- **amount** (double) - The amount of threat to add.

**Returns:** boolean - True if threat was successfully added, false otherwise.

#### `reduceThreat`

Reduces threat for a target in a mob's threat table.

**Method:** N/A (Instance Method)

**Parameters:**
- **mob** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The entity representing the mob.
- **target** (org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity) - The living entity to reduce threat from.
- **amount** (double) - The amount of threat to reduce.

**Returns:** boolean - True if threat was successfully reduced, false otherwise.

#### `taunt`

Sets TARGET to 110% of the mob's target's threat value, and forces the mob to switch targets.

**Method:** N/A (Instance Method)

**Parameters:**
- **mob** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The entity representing the mob.
- **target** (org.bukkit.entity.LivingEntity) - The living entity to taunt.

**Returns:** boolean - True if taunt was successful, false otherwise.

#### `castSkill` (Overloaded Methods)

Casts a specified skill on an entity with various parameter options.

**Method:** N/A (Instance Method)

**Parameters (Examples):
- **e** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The entity casting the skill.
- **skillName** (String) - The name of the skill to cast.
- **power** (float) - The power of the skill.
- **transformer** (Consumer<SkillMetadata>) - A consumer to transform skill metadata.
- **trigger** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The entity that triggered the skill.
- **origin** (org.bukkit.Location) - The origin location of the skill.
- **eTargets** (Collection<org.bukkit.entity.Entity>) - A collection of entity targets.
- **lTargets** (Collection<org.bukkit.Location>) - A collection of location targets.

**Returns:** boolean - True if the skill was cast successfully, false otherwise.

#### `getMythicMob`

Retrieves a MythicMob object by its type name.

**Method:** N/A (Instance Method)

**Parameters:**
- **type** (String) - The name of the MythicMob type.

**Returns:** MythicMob - The MythicMob object if found, null otherwise.

#### `getMythicMobInstance`

Retrieves the ActiveMob instance for a given Bukkit entity.

**Method:** N/A (Instance Method)

**Parameters:**
- **e** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The Bukkit entity.

**Returns:** ActiveMob - The ActiveMob instance if the entity is a MythicMob, null otherwise.

#### `isMythicMob` (Overloaded Methods)

Checks if a given UUID or Bukkit entity represents a MythicMob.

**Method:** N/A (Instance Method)

**Parameters:**
- **u** (UUID) - The UUID to check.
- **l** (org.bukkit.entity.Entity) - The Bukkit entity to check.

**Returns:** boolean - True if the entity is a MythicMob, false otherwise.

```
